\displaystyle\max_{i,i^{\prime}}\bigg\|\big[\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ii^{\prime}}\big)^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big]\bigg(\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{Q}_{i^{\prime}i}+\bm{x}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{y}_{i}\bm{Q}_{ii^{\prime}}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|.

Therefore, by the generalized Bernstein inequality (Theorem 2.8.1 of
64
), it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\big[\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ii^{\prime}}\big)^{2}-1\big]\bigg(\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{Q}_{i^{\prime}i}+\bm{x}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{y}_{i}\bm{Q}_{ii^{\prime}}\bigg)\bigg|\lesssim t\sqrt{Ln}\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|

with probability at least
1-\exp(-c\min(t^{2}n,t\sqrt{Ln}))
. Letting
t\asymp\sqrt{n}r
shows that with probability at least
1-\exp\big(-c\min\{n^{2}r^{2},n^{2}\sqrt{Lr}\big)\geq 1-\exp(-cnr)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\big[\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ii^{\prime}}\big)^{2}-1\big]\bigg(\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{Q}_{i^{\prime}i}+\bm{x}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{y}_{i}\bm{Q}_{ii^{\prime}}\bigg)\bigg|\lesssim nr\sqrt{L}\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}.

–

The second term
. We note that for
i\neq i^{\prime}
,
j\neq i^{\prime},k\neq i
, the second term is a U-statistic in
\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}
. By the decoupling inequality (Theorem 3.4.1 of
31
), it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i\neq i^{\prime}}\sum_{j\neq i^{\prime},k\neq i}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bigg|>t\bigg\}\leq C\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i\neq i^{\prime}}\sum_{j\neq i^{\prime},k\neq i}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{\widetilde{N}}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bigg|>Ct\bigg\},

where
\bm{\widetilde{N}}^{(l)}
is an independent copy of
\bm{N}^{(l)}
.
Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}:=\max_{l}\|\bm{N}^{(l)}\|\leq C\sigma\sqrt{nr}.

By a union bound, we note that
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-L\exp(-cnr)
. We observe that on the event
\mathcal{E}
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\bm{\widetilde{N}}_{ij}^{(l)}\bm{x}_{i}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{i^{\prime}\neq i,j}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}L\max_{l,i}\sum_{j}\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}\neq k,i}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}L\max_{l}\max_{i}\sum_{j}\bigg\|e_{j}^{\top}\bm{Q}\mathcal{H}\big(\mathcal{P}^{-i}\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}\big)\big)\bm{y}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}L\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}\max_{l}\max_{i}\bigg\|\mathcal{H}\big(\mathcal{P}^{-i}\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}\big)\big)\bm{y}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}L\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}\max_{l}\|\bm{N}^{(l)}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{4}Lnr\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2},

where
\mathcal{P}^{-i}
removes the
i
’th row of
\bm{N}^{(l)}
and
\mathcal{H}
is the hollowing operator. In the final line we used the fact that the spectral norm of any submatrix is bounded by the spectral norm of the matrix itself.
Consequently, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i\neq i^{\prime}}\sum_{j\neq i^{\prime},k\neq i}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{\widetilde{N}}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bigg|>Ct\sigma^{2}\sqrt{Lnr}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}\cap\mathcal{E}\bigg\}\leq 2\exp(-ct^{2}).

If
t\asymp\sqrt{nr}
, then we have that with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{i\neq i^{\prime}}\sum_{j\neq i^{\prime},k\neq i}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{y}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{\widetilde{N}}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{i^{\prime}k}\bigg|\leq C\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}.

Combining these bounds and taking a union bound over
\bm{x}
and
\bm{y}
in an
\varepsilon
-net and applying Lemma 4.4.1 of
64
(adjusting constants if necessary) shows that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{H}\bigg(\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}L\bm{Q}\bigg)\bigg\|\lesssim nr\sqrt{L}\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}

with probability at least
1-\exp\big(-cnr\big).

•

The diagonal
. We note that since the matrix in question is diagonal, we need only bound it for fixed
i
(and take a union bound over
i
). We have that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\sum_{j,k}\bm{N}_{ij}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}_{ki}^{(l)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{j\neq k}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ki}+\sum_{l}\sum_{j}\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}\big)^{2}\bm{Q}_{jj}.

By a similar argument to the previous part of the proof, by the decoupling inequality we can show that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{j\neq k}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}\bm{Q}_{jk}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ki}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\sigma^{2}\sqrt{Ln}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2}n)
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\sum_{j}\bigg(\big(\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ij}\big)^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\mathbb{I}_{\{i=j\}}\bigg)\bm{Q}_{jj}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\sqrt{Ln}\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-c\min(t^{2}n,t\sqrt{Ln}))
. The rest of the argument is exactly the same as the previous part, which yields the proof of
Eq.

14b
.

To prove (
14c
), we first let
\bm{x},\bm{y}
be deterministic unit vectors of dimension
n-r
and
r
respectively. Then we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\langle\bm{z}^{(l)}_{1},\bm{z}^{(l)}_{2}\rangle,

where
\bm{z}^{(l)}_{1}
and
\bm{z}^{(l)}_{2}
are uncorrelated
r
-dimensional random vectors with covariances equal to
\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{r}
respectively. The
\psi_{1}
norm is bounded by
\sigma^{2}r
, and we can apply Bernstein’s inequality for sums of subexponential random variables to yield that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l}\langle\bm{z}^{(l)}_{1},\bm{z}^{(l)}_{2}\rangle\bigg|>\sigma^{2}t\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-c\min\bigg(\frac{t^{2}}{Lr^{2}},\frac{t}{r}\bigg)\bigg\}.

Therefore, taking
t=C\max\{\sqrt{nL}r,nr\}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{y}\bigg|\leq C\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cn)
. Taking a net over
\bm{x,y}
after adjusting necessary constants completes the proof.

The proof for (
14d
) follows from
Lemma

1
.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

3

Proof.

First, recall that

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\sf L}_{t}&:=-\frac{1}{L}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{1}{L}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\\
&\quad-\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\end{split}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}{\sf L}_{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{L}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle=:T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}.

We bound each term in turn.

•

The term
T_{1}
. The first term satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg(\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg(\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\bigg)

(19)

on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
by (
14a
) and (
14d
), where in the final inequality we have applied Lemma 1 of
22
.

•

The term
T_{2}
. We have that

\displaystyle\|T_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{T}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{S}^{(l)}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{S}^{(l)}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}},

(20)

where we have again used (
14a
) and (
14d
) and Lemma 1 of
22
.

•

The term
T_{3}
. By exactly the same argument as the term
T_{1}
, we have that

\displaystyle\|T_{3}\|_{F}\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg(\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\bigg).

(21)

Combining (
19
),(
20
), and (
21
) shows that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}{\sf L}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\bigg)+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{nr}\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{L}}

where the final bound holds as long
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

We now bound
\|{\sf L}_{t}\|_{F}
. Using the same arguments as the previous bound, we have

\displaystyle\|{\sf L}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg\|_{F}\bigg(\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}+\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}+\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\sqrt{r}\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}

which completes the proof.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

4

Proof.

We have that

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{t}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{1}{L}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}.

(22)

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
by (
14b
) for any matrix
\bm{Q}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg(\bm{Q}+{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q})\bm{I}\bigg)\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}.

By adding and subtracting appropriately, we obtain that

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}{\sf Q}_{t}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\frac{1}{L}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}}_{=:T_{1}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\frac{1}{L}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{UU}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{UU}^{\top}+r\bm{I}_{r}\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}}_{=:T_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}}_{=:T_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{UU}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}r\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}}_{=:T_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{UU}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}}_{=:T_{5}}.

We bound each term in turn.

•

The term
T_{1}
:
By (
14b
) since
{\sf Tr}(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top})={\sf Tr}(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})=r
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|T_{1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top})\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}C\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}},

where the final inequality holds by Lemma 1 of
22
.

•

The term
T_{2}
:
By a similar argument, it holds that

\displaystyle\|T_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{UU}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}+r\bm{I}_{r}\big)\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}C\sigma^{2}\frac{nr^{3/2}}{\sqrt{L}}.

•

The term
T_{3}
:
Following a similar argument,

\displaystyle\|T_{3}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}}.

•

The term
T_{4}
.
We have that

\displaystyle\|T_{4}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{2}\big)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}.

•

The term
T_{5}
.
We now instead invoke (
14c
) to note that

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{UU}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\leq C\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L}.

Combining these inequalities shows that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}{\sf Q}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}}+\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|_{F}\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{C\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L},

where the last inequality holds whenever
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}
, which is assumed.

We now bound
\|{\sf Q}_{t}\|_{F}
. We can rewrite
{\sf Q}_{t}
via

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{L}\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg)\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}+r\bm{I}_{r}\big)\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},

since
(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top})\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}=0
. Therefore, appealing again to (
14b
),

\displaystyle\|{\sf Q}_{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{r}}{L}\bigg\|\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}+r\bm{I}_{r}\big)\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{r}}{L}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}+r\bm{I}_{r}\big)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\sqrt{L}}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr^{2}}{\sqrt{L}}

as required.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

5

Proof of
Lemma

5
.

The upper bound is immediate. For the lower bound, we first note that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})=\lambda_{r}(\cos\Theta(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},\bm{U}))^{2}\geq 1-\|\sin\Theta(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},\bm{U})\|_{F}^{2}\geq\frac{3}{4}

since
\|\sin\Theta(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},\bm{U})\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}
by assumption. Consequently, with respect to the positive semidefinite ordering, it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\succeq\frac{3}{4}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\succeq\frac{3}{4}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bigg(\sum_{l}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\bigg)\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}.

Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\lambda_{r}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bigg(\sum_{l}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\bigg)\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\lambda_{r}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\lambda_{r}\bigg(\sum_{l}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{9}{16}L\lambda^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{L\lambda^{2}}{4}

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Proofs for Asymptotic Normality and Inference

In this section we prove our main asymptotic results; namely
Theorems

5
and
7
, both of which build on
Theorem

2
. In order to prove these results we require the following theorem, which isolates the leading-order term in the asymptotic expansion.

Theorem 9
.

Suppose that the conditions of
Theorem

2
and
Theorem

5
hold. Then we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Res},

where
{\sf Res}
satisfies the bound

\displaystyle{\sf Res}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{4}r^{5/2}n^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)-\exp(-c\sqrt{L})
.

Proof.

See
Section

B.1
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem

9

Proof of
Theorem

9
.

Under the conditions of the theorem, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
we have that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},\bm{U})\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

(23)

We use this bound without additional remarks in the subsequent analysis. Throughout the proof we will suppress the dependence of
\bm{\widehat{U}}
on the time index
t
, and assume only that
t
is taken such that (
23
) holds. We also note that it is immediate that

\displaystyle\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|\leq\frac{1}{L\lambda^{2}},

a fact we will use repeatedly.
In the subsequent analysis we will define the orthogonal matrix

\mathcal{O}=\argmin_{\mathcal{OO}^{\top}=\bm{I}_{r}}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\|_{F};

the Frobenius-optimal orthogonal matrix aligning
\bm{\widehat{U}}
and
\bm{U}
.
Finally, by
Eq.

23
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}};\qquad\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}},

which follows from Lemma 1 of
22
.

•

Step 1: Initial Expansion
. First, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}=\bm{U}

and also that
\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}=\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}
. Thus, using the fact that
\bm{A}^{(l)}=\bm{S}^{(l)}+\bm{N}^{(l)}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}+\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1})+\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}+\sum_{l}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\mathcal{O}-\bm{UR}^{(l)}\big)\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\bigg)\mathcal{O}.

(24)

We therefore expand
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}
via

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)+\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\bigg)\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U})\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)+\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\bigg)\mathcal{O}.

(25)

Define

\displaystyle\bm{G}^{(1)}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1};

\displaystyle\bm{G}^{(2)}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1};

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U})\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O};

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg)+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bigg(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg);

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O};

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle:=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\bigg)\mathcal{O}.

We have thus shown that

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\bm{G}^{(1)}+\bm{G}^{(2)}+T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}.

(26)

In the next steps we will bound the residual quantities.

•

Step 2: Bounding
T_{1}
through
T_{3}
.

First, in order to bound
T_{1}
through
T_{3}
, we first require the following bound controlling the approximation of
\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}
to
(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}
.

Lemma 6
.

Suppose the conditions of
Theorem

9
hold. Let
\mathcal{O}=\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{\widehat{U}}}
. Then with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}Lr^{3/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{nL}+\sigma r\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\sigma\sqrt{nLr}\lambda_{\max};

(27)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}

(28)

Proof.

See
Section

B.1.1
.
∎

In particular, when
\lambda/\sigma\gg nr^{2}\kappa^{4}/\sqrt{L}
, on the event above, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\|\lesssim\frac{1}{L\lambda^{2}},

(29)

a fact that we will use repeatedly.

First, we note that on
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
, by (
14a
),

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{UU}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\big\|\big\|\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}n}{L\lambda^{2}}.

(30)

Similarly, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
, by
Eq.

14d
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\big)\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{nL}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nr}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}L}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}n\sqrt{r}\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{2}L}

(31)

Thus, we have shown that

\displaystyle\|T_{1}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}n}{L\lambda^{2}}.

(32)

Next, we bound the term
T_{2}
. First, we have that by (
14c
) and
Lemma

6
,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\|\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}rn\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{3}r^{3/2}n^{3/2}}{L^{3/2}}.

Similarly, using (
14d
) instead,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nL}\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}n\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}.

Combining these bounds, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
,

\displaystyle\|T_{2}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}n\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}.

(33)

Analyzing
T_{3}
is slightly more complicated and requires additional expansion, so this analysis is deferred to the following lemma.

Lemma 7
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

9
,
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|T_{3}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}r^{2}\kappa}{\lambda^{3}L}.

Proof.

See
Section

B.1.2
.
∎

Thus, we have shown that with high probability,

\displaystyle\|T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}r^{2}\kappa}{\lambda^{3}L}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{2}L}.

•

Step 3: Bounding
T_{4}
.

We now turn to the quantity
T_{4}
. This quantity is defined via

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle:=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\bigg)\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}\widehat{R}}}^{\top}-\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\bigg)\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=-L\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}})\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}.

(34)

Here
\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}
is understood as the Riemannian gradient of
h
with respect to the Grassmann manifold. We will show that on a good event, this gradient is small deterministically. As a first step we demonstrate a form of geodesic strong convexity and smoothness of
h
.

Lemma 8
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

9
, there exists an event
\mathcal{E}_{\nabla^{2}h}
satisfying
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{\nabla^{2}h})\geq 1-\exp(-cnr)
such that

\displaystyle 3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\geq\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]\geq\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}

uniformly over all
\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
satisfying
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}=\bm{I}_{r}
,
\|\sin\Theta(\bm{W},\bm{U})\|_{F}\leq\frac{C_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
, and
\bm{\Xi}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
satisfying
\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{W}=0
. Here
\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}
is the Riemannian Hessian of
h
defined on the Grassmann Manifold
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}=\bm{I}_{r}
.

Proof.

See
Section

B.1.3
.
∎

By taking
C_{0}>100C_{1}
in
Theorem

2
, for all iterations
t
larger than
C\log\big(\frac{\lambda/\sigma\sqrt{L}}{C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{nr}}\big)
it holds that every iterate lies strictly in the interior of the set
\{\bm{W}:\|\sin\Theta(\bm{W},\bm{U})\|_{F}\leq\frac{C_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\}
. In addition,
Algorithm

1
can be written as

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t+1}={\sf SVD}_{r}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-\eta\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\bigg),

so that
{\sf SVD}_{r}
is a retraction of the iterate onto the Grassmann manifold. Thus,
Lemma

8
implies that
h
is smooth along the retraction curve

\displaystyle s\mapsto h\big({\sf SVD}_{r}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}-s\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\big),

and thus

\displaystyle h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})+\langle\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t}),-\eta\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\rangle+\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-\eta\bigg(1-\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\eta}{2}\bigg)\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|_{F}^{2}.

(35)

The coefficient
\eta\big(1-\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\eta}{2}\big)
is strictly positive for
\eta
chosen as in
Theorem

2
. Letting
t_{0}
be the smallest value
t
such that
\|\sin\Theta(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t_{0}},\bm{U})\|_{F}\leq\frac{2C_{1}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
and applying the above result up to time
T
yields

\displaystyle\eta\bigg(1-\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\eta}{2}\bigg)\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{T-1}\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|_{F}^{2}\leq h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t_{0}})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{T})\leq h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t_{0}})<\infty.

Letting
T\to\infty
shows that
\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|\to 0
. Therefore, there is at least one convergent subsequence by compactness of the Grassmann manifold. Let this limit point be denoted as
\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty}
. Since the Riemannian gradient is continuous, we must have that
\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})=0
. Thus, every limit point of the iterates must be stationary.

Next, note that
\frac{C_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\ll 1
under the conditions of
Theorem

9
. Thus, there is a geodesically convex ball
\mathcal{B}(\bm{U})
satisfying

\displaystyle\big\{\bm{W}:\|\sin\Theta(\bm{W},\bm{U})\|_{F}\leq\frac{2C_{1}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\big\}\subset\mathcal{B}(\bm{U})\subset\big\{\bm{W}:\|\sin\Theta(\bm{W},\bm{U})\|_{F}\leq\frac{C_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\big\}.

If necessary we may increase the value of
C_{0}
. Then every geodesic lies within
\mathcal{B}(\bm{U})
. Hence, when the event in
Lemma

8
holds, the function
h
is geodesically strongly convex and smooth within
\mathcal{B}(\bm{U})
. Thus, by Corollary 11.22 of
12
, every stationary point is a global minimizer within this set.

Now, suppose that
\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty}
is a local minimizer of
h
within the ball of radius
\frac{2C_{1}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
. By Lemma 11.28 of
12
, it holds that

\displaystyle h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\lambda^{2}}\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|_{F}^{2}.

Thus, plugging this into (
35
), we have

\displaystyle h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t+1})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{2}\bigg(1-\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\eta}{2}\bigg)\big[h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})\big]

\displaystyle=\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{2}+\frac{3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\lambda^{2}\eta^{2}}{4}\bigg)\big[h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})\big]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{4}\bigg)\big[h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})\big],

where we recall that
\eta\leq c_{\eta}/\lambda_{\max}^{2}
, and provided that
c_{\eta}
is sufficiently small.
Next,
Lemma

8
and Corollary 10.47 of
12
implies that
\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\cdot)
is
3\lambda_{\max}^{2}
-Lipschitz, and hence

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 9\lambda_{\max}^{4}{\sf dist}(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t},\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty})^{2}.

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{\lambda^{2}}(h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{t})-h(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq 18\lambda_{\max}^{2}\kappa^{2}\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{4}\bigg)^{t-t_{0}}\frac{C\kappa\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}},

where the penultimate inequality is due to restricted strong convexity in
Lemma

8
(see the argument leading to Equation 11.19 in
12
).
Thus, plugging in this bound to the expression (
34
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|T_{4}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 18L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\kappa^{2}\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{8}\bigg)^{t-t_{0}}\frac{C\kappa\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\|\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{4}\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{4}\bigg)^{t-t_{0}}.

Thus, when
t-t_{0}\geq\log\bigg(\frac{C\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg(1-\frac{\lambda^{2}\eta}{4}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg)
(which is guaranteed by the conditions of
Theorem

5
) we have that

\displaystyle\|T_{4}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{2}L}.

(36)

•

Step 4: Expanding the square and controlling small-order terms.

From (
26
),

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\bm{G}^{(2)}\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle+2\sum_{i=1}^{4}\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{i}\rangle+2\sum_{i=1}^{4}\langle\bm{G}^{(2)},T_{i}\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{4}\|T_{i}\|_{F}^{2}.

We will bound all but the first term.

–

Bounding
\|\bm{G}^{(2)}\|_{F}^{2}
.

Lemma

2
and (
14c
) show that on
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{G}^{(2)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}r^{3}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}.

(37)

where we have used the fact that
L\lesssim n
.

–

Bounding
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle
. Analyzing this quantity is somewhat involved and requires decoupling arguments, so its analysis is contained in the following lemma.

Lemma 9
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

9
, for all
u\leq L^{1/4}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim u\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}

with probability at least
1-r^{2}\exp(-cu^{2})-r\exp(-cn)
.

Proof.

See
Section

B.1.4
.
∎

Thus, by taking
u\asymp L^{1/4}
, as long as
\log(r)\leq c\sqrt{L}
, we have, with probability at least
1-\exp(-c\sqrt{L})
, that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim L^{1/4}\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}nr^{5/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa n^{3/2}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}.

(38)

–

Bounding
\sum_{i}\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{i}\rangle
. For terms
T_{1},T_{2}
, and
T_{4}
, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz. By (
32
),(
33
), and (
36
) we have that

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{i}\rangle|\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{2}L}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{4}n^{3/2}r^{2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}.

For the final term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{3}\rangle
, we also require a more involved argument, which is given in the following result.

Lemma 10
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

9
, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{3}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

Proof.

See
Section

B.1.5
.
∎

Thus, we have that with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn),

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}.

(39)

–

Bounding
\sum_{i}\langle\bm{G}^{(2)},T_{i}\rangle
.
By the argument leading to (
37
) we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{G}^{(2)}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}n}{\lambda^{2}L}.

Therefore, by (
32
), (
33
),
Lemma

7
, and (
36
),

\displaystyle\big|\sum_{i}\langle\bm{G}^{(2)},T_{i}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}n}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}n}{L\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}n\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}+\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}r^{2}\kappa}{\lambda^{3}L}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{4}r^{3}n^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{4}r^{2}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L}+\frac{\sigma^{5}r^{7/2}\kappa n^{5/2}}{\lambda^{5}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}r^{5/2}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}r^{3}\kappa^{4}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{5}r^{7/2}\kappa n^{5/2}}{\lambda^{5}L^{2}}.

(40)

–

Bounding
\sum_{i=1}^{4}\|T_{i}\|_{F}^{2}
. By (
32
), (
33
),
Lemma

7
, and (
36
), we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}\|T_{i}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}n}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}n\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}r^{2}\kappa}{\lambda^{3}L}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{8}r^{3}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{6}n^{3}r^{4}\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{6}L^{2}}.

(41)

Thus, combining (
37
), (
38
), (
39
), (
40
), (
41
), we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Res},

where

\displaystyle{\sf Res}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}r^{3}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa n^{3/2}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}r^{3}\kappa^{4}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{5}r^{7/2}\kappa n^{5/2}}{\lambda^{5}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{8}r^{3}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{6}n^{3}r^{4}\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{6}L^{2}}.

Recall that in
Theorem

9
we assume that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gg\frac{nr^{2}\kappa^{4}}{\sqrt{L}}.

Thus, term-by-term comparison together with the assumption
L\lesssim n
shows that

\displaystyle{\sf Res}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{4}r^{5/2}n^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}.

This completes the proof.
∎

B.1.1
Proof of
Lemma

6

Proof of
Lemma

6
.

We have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)})^{2}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}(\bm{S}^{(l)}+\bm{N}^{(l)})\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}(\bm{S}^{(l)}+\bm{N}^{(l)})\bm{\widehat{U}}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}(\bm{S}^{(l)})^{2}\bm{U}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}(\bm{S}^{(l)})^{2}\bm{U}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg\|_{F}+2\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\mathcal{O}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{U}\mathcal{O}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\big)\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\mathcal{O}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[(\bm{U}\mathcal{O}-\bm{\widehat{U}})^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg\|_{F}+2\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg\|_{F}.

It is straightforward to show that since
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
when
t
is chosen as in the lemma then it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\mathcal{O}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{U}\mathcal{O}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\big)\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\mathcal{O}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[(\bm{U}\mathcal{O}-\bm{\widehat{U}})^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

Therefore, we focus on the remaining two terms containing
\bm{N}^{(l)}
.

•

The Term
\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}
. We note that

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+2\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+2\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i=1}^{5}M_{i}.

We analyze each in turn.

–

The Term
M_{1}
. By (
14b
) we have that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
,

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bigg[\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}+r\bm{I}\bigg)\bigg]\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{2}Lr}{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}L\sigma^{2}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}+r\bm{I}_{n-r}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}Lr.

–

The Term
M_{2}
.
We bound similarly via

\displaystyle M_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg]\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg]\bm{U}\bigg\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}\frac{\sigma^{2}L}{2}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}L

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\sigma^{2}L.

–

The Term
M_{3}
.
We again proceed via a similar argument:

\displaystyle M_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+r\bm{I}\bigg]\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}\frac{\sigma^{2}Lr}{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}Lr

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}Lr.

–

The Term
M_{4}
.
We simply have that

\displaystyle M_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg]\bigg]\bigg)\bm{U}\bigg\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma^{2}L}{2}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg]\bigg]\bigg)\bm{U}\bigg\|+\frac{\sigma^{2}L}{2}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}+\sigma^{2}L\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}L

–

The Term
M_{5}
. First, we note that

\displaystyle M_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg\|.

Define the matrix

\displaystyle\bm{Z}:=\big[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(1)}\bm{U},\cdots,\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(L)}\bm{U}\big]\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times rL}.

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{ZZ}^{\top}\big\|=\|\bm{Z}\|^{2}.

A straightforward
\varepsilon
-net argument shows that
\|\bm{Z}\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{Lr}+\sigma\sqrt{nr}
with probability at least
1-\exp(-nr)
. Consequently,
\|\bm{ZZ}^{\top}\|^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}Lr+\sigma^{2}nr+\sigma^{2}r\sqrt{nL}
.

Combining these bounds shows that with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}Lr+\sigma^{2}Lr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{nL}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}Lr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{nL}.

•

The Term
\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}
. We bound using (
14a
) by

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\big\|\sum_{l}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim C\sigma r\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+C\sigma\sqrt{nLr}\lambda_{\max}

.

Combining all these inequalities, we end up with

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}Lr^{3/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{nL}+\sigma r\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\sigma\sqrt{nLr}\lambda_{\max}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
.

To prove the second bound, we note that

\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top}
has smallest eigenvalue at least
L\lambda^{2}
. Thus, under the assumption that
\lambda/\sigma\gg\frac{\kappa^{3}r^{2}n}{\sqrt{L}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\ll L\lambda^{2},

and hence Weyl’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\sum_{l}\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\bigg)\gtrsim L\lambda^{2}.

Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\sum_{l}\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\bigg)^{-1}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\bigg)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bigg[\big(\bm{\widehat{R}}^{(l)}\big)^{2}-\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\mathcal{O}\bigg]\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}\bigg\{L\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}Lr^{3/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}+\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{nL}+\sigma r\sqrt{n}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\sigma\sqrt{nLr}\lambda_{\max}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{2}L}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{n}\kappa}{\lambda^{3}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{nr}\kappa}{L^{3/2}\lambda^{3}}.

The final bound can be directly verified from the assumptions
L\lesssim n
and
\lambda/\sigma\gg nr^{2}\kappa^{3}/\sqrt{L}
.
∎

B.1.2
Proof of
Lemma

7

Proof.

We start with the full decomposition

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}-\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U})\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i=1}^{9}J_{i}.

(42)

We will bound each of these terms separately and collect the bounds at the end of the proof. However, first recall that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
by (
14b
) it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{Q}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q})\bm{I}\big)\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}

for all matrices
\bm{Q}
of rank at most
2r
. We will apply this result repeatedly without reference. For various choices of the matrix
\bm{Q}
above, we refer to
\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{Q}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q})\bm{I}\big)
as the
centering term
. In the subsequent analysis, either the centering term will vanish, or we will include it directly.

•

The Term
J_{1}
: By adding and subtracting the centering term, we have that

\displaystyle\|J_{1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}L\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big]\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}L\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|\big\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}L\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|\big\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\|_{F}\big\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}L\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|_{F}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big]\bigg)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{3}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|+\sigma^{2}L\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{3}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}

(43)

•

The Term
J_{2}
: Since
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{U}=0
, it holds that
{\sf Tr}(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top})={\sf Tr}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U})=0
. Then the centering term vanishes and hence

\displaystyle\|J_{2}\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}.

(44)

•

The Term
J_{3}
. Similar to the prior argument, the centering term vanishes, and hence

\displaystyle\|J_{3}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\quad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}.

(45)

•

The Term
J_{4}
. We note that only the trace part of the centering term does not vanish, which gives

\displaystyle\|J_{4}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}L{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}}\big]\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma^{2}Lr}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}},

(46)

where we have observed that
\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{r}
and
{\sf Tr}(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U})=\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}\leq r
.

•

The Term
J_{5}
. As in previous cases, the centering term vanishes (due to the pre and post multiplication by
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}
and
\bm{U}
respectively). Consequently,

\displaystyle\|J_{5}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}.

(47)

•

The Term
J_{6}
. The centering term vanishes, yielding

\displaystyle\|J_{6}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\big\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}.

(48)

•

The Term
J_{7}
. Only the trace term vanishes, yielding

\displaystyle\|J_{7}\|_{F}
