\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}L\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

(49)

•

The Term
J_{8}
. Here the centering term vanishes giving

\displaystyle\|J_{8}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sigma^{2}L\bigg[\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big]\bm{I}\bigg]\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\max\{\sqrt{nLr},nr\}}{\lambda^{2}L}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{2}L}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

(50)

•

The term
J_{9}
. For
J_{9}
we instead invoke (
14c
) to yield

\displaystyle\|J_{9}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U})\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg\|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}\|_{F}\|\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

(51)

Define

\displaystyle\delta:=\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}.

Combining (
43
),(
44
),(
45
),(
46
),(
47
),(
48
),(
49
),(
50
), and (
51
), we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\|J_{i}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{3}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)+\delta^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}+\bigg)+\delta\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}\sqrt{L}}{\lambda^{2}L}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}},

where the final bound is due to the assumption that
L\lesssim n
.
This completes the proof.
∎

B.1.3
Proof of
Lemma

8

Proof.

Define

\displaystyle h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{S}^{(l)}-\bm{WW}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{WW}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2},

which is the noiseless loss function. Then for any
\bm{\Xi}
satisfying
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}=0
,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\big(\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\big)^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}.

(52)

Thus, we have the decomposition

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}

\displaystyle h(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{L}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]-\frac{2}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]-\frac{2}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}.

Define

\displaystyle{\sf L}_{\nabla^{2}h}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{L}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]-\frac{2}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg];

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{\nabla^{2}h}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2},

(53)

the first being linear in
\bm{N}^{(l)}
and the second being quadratic in
\bm{N}^{(l)}
. Then we have that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]=\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]+{\sf L}_{{\nabla^{2}h}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})+{\sf Q}_{\nabla^{2}h}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi}).

First we bound
\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]
and then we upper bound the two noise terms.

Bounding
\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}
:

Observe that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\bigg]\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\lambda^{2}.

Without loss of generality we may assume that
\bm{W}=\bm{W}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{W},\bm{U}}
since
h(\bm{W})=h(\bm{W}\mathcal{O})
for any orthogonal
\mathcal{O}
. Then we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{W}-\bm{U}\|_{F}\leq R:=C\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}},

where
C
is some sufficiently large fixed constant. Now suppose that
\bm{\Xi}
satisfies
\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{W}=0
. Then the first term on the right hand side of (
52
) satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\frac{1}{L}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg).

We have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}-\bm{I}_{r}\|_{F}\leq\|\bm{W}-\bm{U}\|_{F}\leq R.

Thus, for any
l
, since
\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\|\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\|(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{W})^{2}-(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{W}-\bm{R}^{(l)})\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{W}\|_{F}+\|\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{W}-\bm{R}^{(l)})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{\max}\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{W}-\bm{R}^{(l)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{\max}\bigg(2\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}-\bm{I}\|_{F}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}\|+\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}-\bm{I}\|_{F}^{2}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 6R\lambda_{\max}^{2}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\lambda^{2}-6R\lambda_{\max}^{2}\bigg).

Similarly

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}.

We now upper bound the remaining three terms on the right hand side of (
52
). First, we note that since
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}=0
, then
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}=(\bm{U}-\bm{W})^{\top}\bm{\Xi}
and hence
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}\leq R\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\leq R^{2}\|\bm{\Xi}\|^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W})^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\bm{\Xi}\|^{2}R^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\|\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\Xi}\|^{2}R^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}.

Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\lambda^{2}-6R\lambda_{\max}^{2}-3R^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\bigg)\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}

as long as
\frac{\kappa^{3}\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\ll 1
. In addition,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h^{{\sf clean}}(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]\leq 2\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}.

Upper bounding
{\sf L}_{{\nabla^{2}h}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})
. We bound only the first term; the other terms are similar. The first term of
{\sf L}_{{\nabla^{2}h}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})
is of the form

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\langle\bm{N}^{(l)},\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\rangle.

Let
\bm{Q}_{1}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}
be any fixed rank at most
r
matrices with Frobenius norm at most 1. Then a standard Hoeffding inequality argument shows that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\langle\bm{N}^{(l)},\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}\sigma}{\sqrt{L}}t

with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2})
. Taking a union bound and applying a simple net argument shows that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\langle\bm{N}^{(l)},\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
. Applying this argument to each linear quantity shows that

\displaystyle|{\sf L}_{\nabla^{2}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})|\lesssim\|\bm{\Xi}\|^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\max}\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
, uniformly over all matrices
\bm{\Xi}
and
\bm{W}
.

Upper bounding
{\sf Q}_{{\nabla^{2}h}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})
. We bound each term in turn, though the argument is similar. First, for any fixed
\bm{Q}_{1}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}
of rank at most
r
, define the process

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2}):=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg].

It is straightforward to check that
\mathbb{E}{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big[{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{Q}_{2}^{\top})+{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q}_{1}){\sf Tr}(\bm{Q}_{2})\big].
Note that
{\sf Tr}(\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{Q}_{2}\bm{N}^{(l)})={\sf Vec}(\bm{N}^{(l)})^{\top}(\bm{Q}_{1}^{\top}\otimes\bm{Q}_{2}){\sf Vec}(\bm{N}^{(l)})
(possibly up to transposes depending on vectorization convention, but this does not change the argument). Therefore, up to potential rescaling due to repetition, the quantity
\mathcal{N}(\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2})
is a quadratic form for the random variable
{\sf Vec}(\bm{N}^{(l)})
. Thus, the Hanson-Wright inequality (Theorem 6.2.1 of
64
) gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\mathcal{N}(\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2})|\geq t\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-cL\min\bigg(\frac{t^{2}}{\sigma^{4}\|\bm{Q}_{1}\otimes\bm{Q}_{2}\|_{F}^{2}},\frac{t}{\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}_{1}\otimes\bm{Q}_{2}\|}\bigg)\bigg\}\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-cL\min\big(\frac{t^{2}}{\sigma^{4}},\frac{t}{\sigma^{2}}\big)\bigg\}.

Take
t\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}
. Then with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}(\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2})|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L},

where we have implicitly used the fact that
L\lesssim n
. Taking a union bound over all
\bm{Q}_{1}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}
in an
\varepsilon
-net shows that this bound holds uniformly over all
\bm{Q}_{1}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}
in the net. Define

\displaystyle M:=\sup_{\|\bm{Q}_{1}\|_{F}=1,\|\bm{Q}_{2}\|=1}|\mathcal{N}(\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2})|,

where the supremum is over rank at most
r
matrices. Let
\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{1},\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{2}
be points in the nets such that they are
\varepsilon
-close to the maximizers
\bm{\check{Q}}_{1},\bm{\check{Q}}_{2}
. Then we have that

\displaystyle M=|\mathcal{N}(\bm{\check{Q}}_{1},\bm{\check{Q}}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathcal{N}(\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{1},\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{2})|+|\mathcal{N}(\bm{\check{Q}}_{1}-\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{1},\bm{\check{Q}}_{2}-\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{2})|+|\mathcal{N}(\bm{\check{Q}}_{1}-\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{1},\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{2})|+|\mathcal{N}(\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{1},\bm{\check{Q}}_{2}-\bm{\widetilde{Q}}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sigma^{2}nr}{L}+8\varepsilon M,

where we have implicitly used the fact that a matrix of rank at most
2r
with Frobenius norm at most
\varepsilon
can be written as a sum of two rank at most
r
matrices with Frobenius norm at most
\varepsilon
. Consequently, we have that
M\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}
by taking
\varepsilon
appropriately.

We now apply this result to each term in the definition of
{\sf Q}_{{\nabla^{2}h}}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})
. We have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}]+

\displaystyle{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}]{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\big]\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}.

We also have that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big[{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}]+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}]{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\top}\big]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\big(\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}r\big)\lesssim\sigma^{2}r\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}.

Similarly, the second term in (
53
) satisfies

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg[{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\big]+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\big]{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{\Xi}\bm{W}^{\top}\big]\bigg]\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}.

The expectation satisfies
{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{W}\big]=\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2},
and
{\sf Tr}[\bm{W}\bm{\Xi}^{\top}]=0
since
\bm{\Xi}^{\top}\bm{W}=0
. The remaining two terms are similar, with centering term equal to zero and
r\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}
respectively. Thus, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
, we have that

\displaystyle|{\sf Q}_{\nabla^{2}h}(\bm{W},\bm{\Xi})|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}.

Putting it all together.
Thus, we have shown that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}-C\frac{\lambda_{\max}\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}-C\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}\bigg)\geq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4}

which holds under the assumption that
\lambda/\sigma\geq C\frac{\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{{\sf Riemannian}}h(\bm{W})[\bm{\Xi},\bm{\Xi}]\leq\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(2\lambda_{\max}^{2}+C\frac{\lambda_{\max}\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\sqrt{L}}+C\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{L}\bigg)\leq 3\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{F}^{2}.

This completes the proof.
∎

B.1.4
Proof of
Lemma

9

Proof.

First, we have that

\displaystyle\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{l^{\prime}}{\sf Tr}\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{l}\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-2}\bigg]_{jj}.

We can view the summation above as a vector-valued (asymmetric)
U
-statistic computed from the matrix observations
\{\bm{N}^{(l)}\}_{l=1}^{L}
. We will bound it for fixed
j\in[r]
and take a union bound.

Define the vector

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}:=\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-2}\big)_{\cdot j}.

Then for fixed
j\in[r]
we can write the inner product as

\displaystyle\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l\neq l^{\prime}}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle+\sum_{l}\big\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\big)_{j\cdot},\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\big\rangle=:K_{1}+K_{2}.

We analyze each term separately.

•

The term
K_{1}
:
By Theorem 3.4.1 of
31
(using an order-two decoupling inequality with an appropriate kernel
h
),
it suffices to consider the case where
\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}
is independent from
\bm{N}^{(l)}
. Conditioning on
\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}
,
K_{1}
is a sum of independent random variables. Its
\psi_{2}
norm satisfies

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq L\sigma^{2}\max_{l^{\prime}}\|\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\|^{2}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\|^{2}.

Therefore, by Hoeffding’s inequality it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\lesssim t\sigma\sqrt{L}\lambda_{\max}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\|

with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2})
.
Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}:=\bigcap_{j=1}^{r}\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{A}^{(l)}_{j}\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}\bigg\}.

(54)

On the event (
54
) it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\lesssim t\sigma\sqrt{L}\lambda_{\max}\frac{\sigma^{2}r\max\{\sqrt{nL},n\}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}.

Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot}

\displaystyle,\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle\bigg|>Ct\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\big)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\rangle\bigg|>Ct\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}\cap\mathcal{E}\bigg\}+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp(-ct^{2})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}).

By
Lemma

2
, the event
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
by (
14c
).

•

The term
K_{2}
.
The term
K_{2}
is directly a sum of independent random variables. Moreover, we have that

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg]_{jk}\bigg[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)^{-2}\bigg]_{kj}.

For fixed
j
and
k
this is a sum of
L
independent random variables with bounded
\psi_{2/3}
norm.
By Lemma 7 of
35
, with probability at least
1-\delta
it holds that

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim r\sqrt{L}\max_{j,k}\bigg\|\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg]_{jk}\bigg[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)^{-2}\bigg]_{kj}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2/3}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}

\displaystyle\quad+r\max_{j,k}\bigg\|\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg]_{jk}\bigg[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)^{-2}\bigg]_{kj}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2/3}}\big(\log(1/\delta)\big)^{3/2}

Therefore, it suffices to bound the Orlicz
\psi_{2/3}
norm of the random variable above. We have

\displaystyle\bigg\|

\displaystyle\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg]_{jk}\bigg[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)^{-2}\bigg]_{kj}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2/3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\bigg[\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bigg]_{jk}\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\bigg\|\bigg[\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)^{-2}\bigg]_{kj}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}n\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{r}\frac{1}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}n\kappa\sqrt{r}}{L^{2}\lambda^{3}}.

Therefore, letting
t=\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}
, by taking a union bound for each
k
, we have that with probability at least
1-r\exp(-ct^{2})
it holds that

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+t^{3}\frac{\sigma^{3}n\kappa r^{3/2}}{L^{2}\lambda^{3}}.

In particular, as long as
t\leq L^{1/4}
it holds that

\displaystyle K_{2}\lesssim t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}.

Combining these two inequalities and bounding the summation over
j
by
r
times the maximum entry and taking a union bound shows that for all
t\leq L^{1/4}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},\bm{G}^{(2)}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r^{2}}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}+t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}n\kappa r^{5/2}}{L^{3/2}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t\frac{\kappa\sigma^{3}\sqrt{n}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{3}L}\max\{1,\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\}

with probability at least
1-r^{2}\exp(-ct^{2})-r\exp(-cn)
.
∎

B.1.5
Proof of
Lemma

10

Proof.

First, according to the same decomposition in (
42
), we have that

\displaystyle\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},T_{3}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{9}\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{i}\rangle,

where we have that

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{5}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{6}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{7}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{8}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle J_{9}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U})\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}.

We will bound each of these terms in turn. Let
\mathcal{E}_{\bm{G}}
denote the event that
\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
, which can be shown to happen with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
through a similar argument as in the proof of
Lemma

1
in
Section

A.1
. For some of the subsequent terms we require additional technical arguments, and for some terms we simply apply Cauchy-Schwarz.

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{1}\rangle
.
We have by Cauchy-Schwarz that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{1}\rangle\bigg|\leq\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}\|J_{1}\|_{F}.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{G}}}
it holds that
\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}
. In addition, by the bound (
43
), we have that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}

\displaystyle\|J_{1}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3},

which gives that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{1}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{4}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{4}.

(55)

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{2}\rangle
.
Again we use Cauchy Schwarz and the bound (
44
), to obtain

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{2}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}\|J_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\asymp\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}\lambda^{2}},

(56)

which again holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\bm{G}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{{\sf good}}
.

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{3}\rangle
.
Again we use Cauchy-Schwarz and the bound
Eq.

45
to yield

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{3}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{G}^{(1)}\|_{F}\|J_{3}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}\lambda^{2}}

(57)

which matches the bound (
56
).

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{4}\rangle
.
We require a more involved argument.
We first decompose

\displaystyle\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{4}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{l^{\prime}}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}^{\top}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq l^{\prime}}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}\bigg[(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg].

The first term above represents the off-diagonal contribution, and the second term represents the diagonal contribution. We bound both terms in turn.

–

The off-diagonal.

To bound this term we will use an
\varepsilon
-net argument. First, let
\bm{Q}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
be any symmetric positive semidefinite matrix of Frobenius norm at most
1
, and let
\bm{Q}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n-r\times r}
be another matrix of Frobenius norm at most one.
We have that

\displaystyle\sum_{l\neq l^{\prime}}{\sf Tr}

\displaystyle\bigg[(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{l\neq l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\bigg((\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bigg)_{j\cdot},\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\rangle.

Fix an index
j
.
Define

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}:=\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg)_{j\cdot}.

By repeating the argument in the proof of
Lemma

9
in
Section

B.1.4
, through a decoupling argument we can show that with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\bigg((\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bigg)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\lesssim t\sigma\sqrt{L}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{A}_{j}^{(l)}\bigg\|.

We note that by
Eq.

14b
, it holds that with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
, uniformly over
\bm{Q}_{1}
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}-\frac{\sigma^{2}L}{2}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg\|_{F}+\frac{\sigma^{2}L}{2}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}\|\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}+\sigma^{2}L\sqrt{r}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}L\sqrt{r}.

Thus, with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2})-\exp(-cnr)
, uniformly over
\bm{Q}_{1}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\bigg((\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bigg)_{j\cdot},\sum_{l}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\sigma\sqrt{L}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{L\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\sigma^{2}nr\sqrt{L}+\sigma^{2}L\sqrt{r}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp t\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa nr}{\lambda}+t\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa\sqrt{rL}}{\lambda}.

Thus, by taking a union bound over
\bm{Q}_{2}
(and possibly increasing implicit constants in the proof of
Eq.

14b
), with probability at least
1-\exp(-cnr)
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l\neq l^{\prime}}\bigg\langle\bigg((\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-2}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bigg)_{j\cdot},\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa n^{3/2}r^{3/2}}{\lambda}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa\sqrt{nL}r}{\lambda}.

In particular, since this term is linear in
\bm{Q}_{i}
, by (
29
) it holds that with this same probability

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r}\bigg|

\displaystyle\sum_{l\neq l}\bigg\langle\bigg((\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l^{\prime})}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l^{\prime})}\bigg)_{j\cdot},\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim r\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa n^{3/2}r^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa\sqrt{n}r}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{L}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{3/2}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa n^{3/2}r^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa\sqrt{n}r}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{L}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}nr^{3}}{\lambda^{4}L}.

–

The diagonal.
We now consider the diagonal term. Instead of a net argument, we will consider the particular choices
\bm{Q}_{1}=\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{l}{\sf Tr}

\displaystyle\bigg[(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq L\max_{l}\|\bm{N}^{(l)}\|^{3}\|(\bm{\mathcal{RR}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\|\|\bm{U}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{U}^{\top}\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Q}_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\sigma^{3}n^{3/2}\frac{\kappa}{L\lambda}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{1}{L\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{4}n^{2}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}.

where we used the fact that
\|\bm{Q}_{2}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{1}{L\lambda^{2}}
when
\bm{Q}_{2}=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\big(\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}\bm{\mathcal{\widehat{R}}}^{\top}\big)^{-1}\mathcal{O}
when (
29
) holds, together with the high probability bound
\|\bm{N}^{(l)}\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{n}
. These bounds hold cumulatively with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
.

Thus, we have shown that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{4}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}nr^{3}}{\lambda^{4}L}+\frac{\sigma^{4}n^{2}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{r}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}nr^{3}}{\lambda^{4}L}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}},

(58)

since
L\lesssim n
.

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{5}\rangle
. Here again we use Cauchy-Schwarz and the bound (
47
) to yield

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{5}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr^{3/2}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{5}\kappa^{3}n^{5/2}r^{3}}{\lambda^{5}L^{2}}.

(59)

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{6}\rangle.
Again we apply Cauchy-Schwarz with the bound (
48
) to yield

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{6}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg[\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{5}\kappa^{3}n^{5/2}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{5}L^{4}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{4}L}.

(60)

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{7}\rangle
. By a completely identical argument to the term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{4}\rangle
, we can derive the exact same bound, yielding

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{7}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}

(61)

which holds with the same probability as in (
58
).

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{8}\rangle
. The same argument as for the term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{4}\rangle
applies verbatim with different choices of
\bm{Q}_{1}
and
\bm{Q}_{2}
, yielding the bound

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{8}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}.

(62)

•

The term
\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{9}\rangle
.
By Cauchy-Schwarz and (
51
),

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{9}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\frac{\sigma^{2}rn}{L\lambda^{2}}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}r^{2}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}.

(63)

Combining
Eqs.

55
,
56
,
57
,
58
,
59
,
60
,
61
,
62
and
63
, we have that with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{9}\langle\bm{G}^{(1)},J_{i}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{4}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{4}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\bigg)^{3}\frac{\sigma^{2}nr}{\sqrt{L}\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{5}\kappa^{3}n^{5/2}r^{3}}{\lambda^{5}L^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{5}\kappa^{3}n^{5/2}r^{5/2}}{\lambda^{5}L^{4}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}nr}{\lambda^{4}L}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}r^{2}n^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}},

where we have used the assumption that
L\lesssim n
and
\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}\lesssim 1
. This completes the proof.
∎

Proof of
Theorem

5

Proof of
Theorem

5
.

First invoke
Theorem

9
to have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle={\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg)+{\sf Res},

where, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)-\exp(-c\sqrt{L})
,

\displaystyle{\sf Res}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{4}r^{5/2}n^{3/2}}{\lambda^{3}L^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{7/2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{3/2}}.

We note that

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}\gtrsim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda^{2}L}.

Thus, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cn)-\exp(-c\sqrt{L})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{{\sf Res}}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa^{4}r^{2}n}{\lambda\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}n^{3/2}r^{3}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}.

We claim that this quantity is
o(1)
. Indeed, the first quantity is
o(1)
by assumption. The second term satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}n^{3/2}r^{3}}{\lambda^{2}\sqrt{L}}\times\sqrt{\frac{n}{L}}\sqrt{\frac{L}{n}}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}n^{2}r^{3}}{\lambda^{2}L}\sqrt{\frac{L}{n}},

which is
o(1)
again with the assumption that
L\lesssim n
.

It therefore suffices to focus on the leading-order term. We further decompose the leading-order term via

\displaystyle{\sf Tr}\bigg(

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg)

\displaystyle={\sf Tr}\bigg(\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg).

The following lemma bounds the second term.

Lemma 11
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

5
, with probability at least
1-\exp(-ct^{2})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}\lesssim(\sqrt{r}+t)\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{L}\lambda}

Proof.

See
Section

B.2.1
.
∎

As a result, we have that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\|\bm{U}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(r+\log(n)+\sqrt{r\log(n)})\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r}{L\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{2}\log(n)}{L\lambda^{2}}.

(64)

We note that
\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}\gtrsim\sigma^{2}\frac{\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda^{2}L}
. Therefore, on the event above it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bm{S}^{(l)}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\lambda^{2}L}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{nr}}\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{2}\log(n)}{L\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\ll 1,

where the final inequality follows from the assumption
(
11
). Thus, it suffices to consider the asymptotics of the remaining term.

The remaining term
can be written via

\displaystyle{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bigg[\sum_{l}\bm{N}^{(l)}

\displaystyle\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}\bm{N}^{(l)}\bigg]\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}=1}^{L}\bm{N}_{ik_{1}}^{(l_{1})}\bm{N}_{ik_{2}}^{(l_{2})}\bigg[\big(\bm{S}^{(l_{1})}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l_{2})}\big)_{k_{1}k_{2}}\bigg].

We will apply the martingale central limit theorem to this quantity.
Define the coefficient

\displaystyle c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}:=\big(\bm{S}^{(l_{1})}\bm{U}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{S}^{(l_{2})}\big)_{k_{1}k_{2}}=e_{k_{1}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bm{U}^{\top}e_{k_{2}}.

Define the random variable

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{ikl}:=\begin{cases}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{kk}&i=k\\
\sqrt{2}\bm{N}^{(l)}_{ik}&i\neq k,\end{cases}

so that each
\bm{Z}_{ikl}
has common variance
\sigma^{2}
and fourth moment
\sigma_{4}^{4}
.
Then the sum can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}}\bm{N}_{ik_{1}}^{(l_{1})}\bm{N}_{ik_{2}}^{(l_{2})}c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{l}c_{ii}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{iil}^{2}+\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}\neq l_{1}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{1}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k\neq i}c_{ik}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{iil}\bm{Z}_{ikl}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{k\neq i}c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{k\neq i}c_{ki}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{1}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k_{1}\neq i}\sum_{k_{2}\neq i,k_{1}}c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k_{1}\neq i}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{k_{1}\neq i}\sum_{k_{2}\neq i,k_{1}}c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{k_{1}\neq i}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{l}c_{ii}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{iil}^{2}+2\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}<l_{1}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{1}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k<i}c_{ik}^{ll}\big(\bm{Z}_{iil}\bm{Z}_{ikl}+\bm{Z}_{kkl}\bm{Z}_{ikl}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}<l_{1}}\sum_{k<i}c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\big(\bm{Z}_{iil_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\bm{Z}_{kkl_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k_{1}<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k_{1}}c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}<l_{1}}\sum_{k_{1}<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k_{1}}c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{k_{1}<i}\sum_{l}(c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i}\sum_{k_{1}<i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}<l_{1}}(c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}+c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{1}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{2}}.

Let
M
denote the random variable above. We now calculate the expectation of
M
. Only the terms with
\bm{Z}_{ik_{1}l_{1}}^{2}
contribute, yielding

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(M)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}c_{ii}^{ll}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{k_{1}<i}\sum_{l}(c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(n-1)}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}e_{i}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\bm{U}^{\top}e_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(n-1)}{2}{\sf Tr}\bigg(\sum_{l}(\bm{R}^{(l)})^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(n-1)}{2}{\sf Tr}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big),

which is nearly the centering term. Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}{\sf Tr}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}}\lesssim\frac{\lambda^{2}L}{\sqrt{nr}}\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{L\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\ll 1.

Therefore, if we can show that
\frac{M-\mathbb{E}(M)}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}}\to\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, then it also holds that
\frac{M-\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{2}{\sf Tr}\big((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\big)}{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n/2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}}\to\mathcal{N}(0,1)
since
r/n\to 0
by (
11
). We therefore focus on the asymptotic normality of
M-\mathbb{E}M
.

We now calculate the variance.
In order to do so, we first define a
\sigma
-algebra
\mathcal{F}_{t}:=\sigma\{\bm{z}_{1},..\bm{z}_{t}\}
, where
\bm{z}_{t}=\bm{Z}_{ikl}
with
t(i,k,l)
as follows. First, define the set

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}:=\{(i,k,l):1\leq i\leq n,1\leq l\leq L,1\leq k\leq i\}.

Then enumerate
\mathcal{I}
via

\displaystyle(1,1,1),(1,2,1),...,(1,i,1),(2,1,1),\dots,(n,i,1),(1,1,2),\dots,

so that first
i
is fixed, then
l
is fixed, and then
k
is fixed (so
k
increases fastest and
i
increases slowest).
Let
M_{T}=M-\frac{\sigma^{2}(n-1)}{2}{\sf Tr}((\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1})
, which is mean-zero.
From this definition it is evident that
M_{T}
is a sum of martingale differences
Y_{t}:=M_{t}-M_{t-1}
with increments given by

\displaystyle Y_{t}

\displaystyle=A_{t}\big(\bm{Z}_{t}^{2}-\sigma^{2}\big)+B_{t}\bm{Z}_{t},

where for
t=t(i,k,l)
we have

\displaystyle A_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}c_{kk}^{ll}&i=k\\
c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}&k<i;\end{cases}

\displaystyle B_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\bigg(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg),&k=i;\\
\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\bigg(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)&k<i.\end{cases}

Note that these coefficients are
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
measurable random variables since any appearance of
\bm{Z}_{i^{\prime}k^{\prime}l^{\prime}}
in the definition of
B_{t}
has at least one index
t
less than
t(i,k,l)
with respect to this enumeration of
\mathcal{I}
.
We compute the conditional variance:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=A_{t}^{2}\big(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})+B_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}+2A_{t}B_{t}\sigma_{3}^{3},

where
\sigma_{4}^{4}
and
\sigma_{3}^{3}
are the noncentral moments of each
\bm{Z}_{t}
. Furthermore, observe that
B_{t}
is linear in previous
\bm{Z}_{s}
(for
s<t
) and
A_{t}
is deterministic. Therefore, since cross-terms cancel,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}A_{t}^{2}\big(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})+\sigma^{2}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}.

Moreover, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}4\sigma^{2}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{k_{2}<i}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}&k=i;\\
\sigma^{2}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}+2\sigma^{2}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}&k<i.\end{cases}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l=1}^{L}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})(c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4\sigma^{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k_{2}<i}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}+\sigma^{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma^{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle=(n-1)\sum_{i,l}(\sigma^{4}_{4}-\sigma^{4})(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k}\sum_{l=1}^{L}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})c_{ii}^{ll}c_{kk}^{ll}

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma^{4}\sum_{i}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}-2\sigma^{4}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k}(c_{ik}^{ll})^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{8}\sum_{i}\sum_{k}\sum_{l_{1}}\sum_{l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{8}\sum_{i}\sum_{k}\sum_{l}(c_{ik}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k_{1}}\sum_{k_{2}}(c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{ll})^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}\sum_{k_{1}}(c_{k_{1}i}^{ll})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i}\sum_{l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,k_{1},k_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}-\sigma^{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{k_{1}i}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l}(c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll})^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,k_{1},l}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}c_{ii}^{ll}

(65)

We now simplify (
65
). First, we note that we have the following identity:

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}}\sum_{k_{2}}(c^{l_{1}l_{2}}_{k_{1}k_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k_{1}}\sum_{k_{2}}\Bigg[e_{k_{1}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bm{U}^{\top}e_{k_{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\bigg(\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg)_{j_{1}j_{2}}\bigg(\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg)_{j_{3}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{k_{1}}\sum_{k_{2}}\bm{U}_{k_{1}j_{1}}\bm{U}_{k_{1}j_{3}}\bm{U}_{k_{2}j_{2}}\bm{U}_{k_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\bigg(\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg)_{j_{1}j_{2}}\bigg(\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg)_{j_{3}j_{4}}\mathbb{I}\{j_{1}=j_{3}\}\mathbb{I}\{j_{2}=j_{4}\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\bigg(\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg)_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bigg\|_{F}^{2},

where we have used the orthogonality of
\bm{U}
above.
Therefore, (
65
) can be written as

(
65
)

\displaystyle=(n-1)(\sigma_{4}^{2}-\sigma^{4})\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}+(\sigma_{4}^{2}-\sigma^{4})\sum_{i,k,l}c_{ii}^{ll}c_{kk}^{ll}+\sigma^{4}\bigg(-2-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{4}-\frac{n}{4}-\frac{n}{4}\bigg)\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{4}\bigg(\frac{n}{2}+2+\frac{1}{2}\bigg)\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{k,k_{1},l}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}c_{kk}^{ll}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{4}\bigg[\frac{n}{2}-1+\frac{1}{8}\bigg]\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}+\sigma^{4}\bigg[\frac{n}{4}-\frac{1}{8}\bigg]\sum_{l}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\bigg[1+\frac{2}{n}\bigg]\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\bigg[\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{4n}\bigg]\sum_{l}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg[(n-1)(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})-\sigma^{4}\big(\frac{n}{2}-2.25\big)\bigg]\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+n\sigma^{4}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2.5}{n}\bigg)\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{4}}{2}\sum_{k,k_{1},l}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}c_{ii}^{ll}.

We will show that under the conditions of
Theorem

5
that this equals
\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}(1+o(1))
. Indeed, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\eqref{tosimplify}-\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{4}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}+C\sigma^{4}n\sum_{l}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\|_{F}^{2}+Cn\sigma^{4}\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+Cn\sigma^{4}\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}+C\sigma^{4}\sum_{i,k_{1},l_{1},l_{2}}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}+C\sigma^{4}\sum_{k,k_{1},l}c_{k_{1}k_{1}}^{ll}c_{ii}^{k_{1}k_{1}}.

Note that
\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\gtrsim\frac{\sigma^{4}nr}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}
. We also have that

\displaystyle\sigma^{4}n\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bm{U}^{\top}e_{i}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{4}n\max_{i}\|e_{i}^{\top}\bm{U}\|^{2}\sum_{i,l_{1},l_{2}}\|e_{i}^{\top}\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{4}n\kappa^{4}r}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}

(66)

\displaystyle\ll\frac{\sigma^{4}nr}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}

as long as
\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\ll\frac{1}{\kappa^{4}}
. The same arguments hold for each of the other terms containing
c_{ii}^{l_{1}l_{2}}
or
c_{ii}^{ll}
as well. In addition,

\displaystyle\sigma^{4}n\sum_{l}\|\bm{R}^{(l)}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l)}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}nr\kappa^{4}}{L^{3}\lambda^{4}}\ll\frac{\sigma^{4}nr}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}

as long as
\frac{\kappa^{4}}{L}\ll 1
. Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}=\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(1+o(1)\bigg).

For simplicity, define

s^{2}=\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}=\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\big(1+o(1)\big).

It suffices to prove the asymptotic normality of
\frac{M_{t}-\mathbb{E}M_{t}}{s}
. We apply the martingale central limit theorem (Lemma 9.12 of
10
). We need to show that

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}}{s^{2}}\xrightarrow{p}1;

(67)

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}\mathbb{I}\{Y_{t}^{2}\geq\varepsilon s^{2}\}}{s^{2}}\to 0

(68)

for all
\varepsilon>0
. To prove (
67
), we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t}A_{t}^{2}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})+B_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}+2A_{t}B_{t}\sigma_{3}^{3}

\displaystyle=(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\Bigg[2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\bigg(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma_{3}^{3}\sum_{i,l}c_{ii}^{ll}\Bigg[2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\bigg(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\big[c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}\big]^{2}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\bigg(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma_{3}^{3}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}[c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}]\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\bigg(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg].

Define

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle:=(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})\sum_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\big[c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}\big]^{2}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4});

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle:=2\sigma_{3}^{3}\sum_{i,l}c_{ii}^{ll}\Bigg[2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\bigg(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma_{3}^{3}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}[c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}]\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\bigg(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle:=\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\Bigg[2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\bigg(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\bigg(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]^{2}.

We analyze
T_{1},T_{2}
, and
T_{3}
separately.

•

The term
T_{1}
.
By a similar argument to (
66
) it holds that
T_{1}\ll s^{2}
.

•

The term
T_{2}
.
As for
T_{2}
, we note that it is mean-zero, and, furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}T_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\sigma_{3}^{6}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{i,l}c_{ii}^{ll}\bigg[2\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bigg]\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+8\sigma_{3}^{6}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}[c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll}]\Bigg(\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}+2\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\sigma_{3}^{6}\sigma^{2}\Bigg(\sum_{i,l}\sum_{l_{2}<l}4(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\frac{1}{2}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\frac{1}{2}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+8\sigma_{3}^{6}\sigma^{2}\Bigg[\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\frac{1}{2}(c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+8\sigma_{3}^{6}\sigma^{2}\Bigg[\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}+4\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll}+c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{8}\sum_{i,l,k,k_{2},l_{2}}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n\sigma^{8}\max_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}\sum_{l,k,k_{2}l_{2}}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n\sigma^{8}\max_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}.

We note that Cauchy-Schwarz implies that

\displaystyle|c_{kk_{1}}^{l_{1}l_{2}}|\leq\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\frac{\kappa^{2}r}{L^{2}\lambda^{2}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle n\sigma^{8}\max_{i,l}(c_{ii}^{ll})^{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{n\sigma^{8}r}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{L^{4}\lambda^{4}}\asymp\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{n\sigma^{8}r^{3}\kappa^{4}}{L^{6}\lambda^{8}}.

As a result, Chebyshev’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|T_{2}|\geq\varepsilon s^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{n\sigma^{8}r^{3}\kappa^{4}}{L^{6}\lambda^{8}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}s^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{n\sigma^{8}r^{3}\kappa^{4}}{L^{6}\lambda^{8}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\frac{L^{4}\lambda^{8}}{r^{2}n^{2}\sigma^{8}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{r\kappa^{4}}{L^{2}n\varepsilon^{2}}\to 0

by
Eq.

11
,
which implies that
T_{2}\to 0
in probability.

•

The term
T_{3}
.
From the previous calculations, we note that
T_{3}
has expected value
\frac{\sigma^{4}n}{2}\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\big(1+o(1)\big)
. We therefore prove the convergence in probability to its expected value. We decompose
T_{3}
via:

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=4\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\big(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\big(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\big(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\big(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\big(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\big(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}^{2}+4\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\big(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\big)^{2}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\sigma^{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\sigma^{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}c_{ii}^{ll_{2}}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}}\bm{Z}_{iil_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}<i\\
k_{2}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}\end{subarray}}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll}c_{ik_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}^{\prime}l_{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}<i\\
k_{2}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}\end{subarray}}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}}c_{ik_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}^{\prime}l_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}

\displaystyle\quad+4\sigma^{2}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}}\bm{Z}_{ikl_{2}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\frac{4}{\sqrt{2}}\sum_{i,l}\sum_{k<i}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}c_{ik}^{l_{3}l}c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}}\bm{Z}_{kkl_{3}}\bm{Z}_{ik_{2}l_{2}}

We claim that we can write this as
\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{QQ}^{\top}\bm{Z}
, where
\bm{Z}
is the vector associated to all unique components of
\bm{Z}_{ikl}
and
\bm{Q}
is a matrix defined as follows. First, recall the set
\mathcal{I}:=\{(i,k,l),k\leq i,l\leq L\}
. Define
\mathcal{J}_{1}:=\{(i,l,l_{2}):l_{2}<l\}
and
\mathcal{J}_{2}:=\{(i,l,k,l_{2}):k<i,\,l_{2}<l\}
, with
\mathcal{J}=\mathcal{J}_{1}\cup\mathcal{J}_{2}
. We write
\bm{Q}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{I}|\times|\mathcal{J}|}
with entries given by

\displaystyle\bm{Q}_{(i^{\prime},k^{\prime},l^{\prime}),(i,l,l_{2})}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}2\sigma\,c_{ii}^{ll_{2}},&(i^{\prime},k^{\prime},l^{\prime})=(i,i,l_{2}),\\[2.84526pt]
\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{2}}\,c_{ik^{\prime}}^{ll},&i^{\prime}=i,\,k^{\prime}<i,\,l^{\prime}=l,\\[2.84526pt]
\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{2}}\,c_{ik^{\prime}}^{ll^{\prime}},&i^{\prime}=i,\,k^{\prime}<i,\,l^{\prime}<l,\\[2.84526pt]
0,&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

\displaystyle\bm{Q}_{(i^{\prime},k^{\prime},l^{\prime}),(i,l,k,l_{2})}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+\sigma c_{ii}^{ll_{2}},&(i^{\prime},k^{\prime},l^{\prime})=(i,k,l_{2}),\\[2.84526pt]
\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{2}}\,c_{ik}^{l^{\prime}l},&(i^{\prime},k^{\prime},l^{\prime})=(k,k,l^{\prime}),\,l^{\prime}\geq l,\\[2.84526pt]
\sigma c_{kk_{2}}^{ll},&i^{\prime}=i,\,k^{\prime}<k,\,l^{\prime}=l,\\[2.84526pt]
2\,\sigma c_{kk_{2}}^{ll^{\prime}},&i^{\prime}=i,\,k^{\prime}<k,\,l^{\prime}<l,\\[2.84526pt]
0,&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

With this definition, we note that
\mathbb{E}T_{3}=\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}
, and, moreover,
T_{3}=\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{QQ}^{\top}\bm{Z}
. Therefore, we may apply the Hanson-Wright inequality to
\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{QQ}^{\top}\bm{Z}
.
In particular, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{QQ}^{\top}\bm{Z}-\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg|>\sigma^{2}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}t\bigg\}\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-c\frac{\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}}{\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}}\min(t^{2},t)\bigg\}.

Thus, convergence in probability is achieved if
\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}/\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}\to\infty
. We now upper bound
\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}
. First, we have that
\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}\leq\|\bm{Q}\|_{1,1}\|\bm{Q}\|_{\infty,\infty}
(these are operator norms with respect to these vector spaces). Note that every entry of
\bm{Q}
satisfies

\displaystyle\max|\bm{Q}_{(i^{\prime}k^{\prime}l^{\prime}),(i,l,l_{2})}|\leq C\sigma\max_{ikll^{\prime}}|c_{ik}^{ll^{\prime}}|.

We simply count the number of nonzeros per row and column of
\bm{Q}
. Note that the only time that
\bm{Q}
has nonzero entries within a row it must be that
i^{\prime}=i
. Thus, we may only vary
k^{\prime}
and
l^{\prime}
, meaning there are at most
O(nL)
nonzero entries. Similarly for the columns, yielding

\displaystyle\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma^{2}n^{2}L^{2}\max_{ikll^{\prime}}|c_{ik}^{ll^{\prime}}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim Cn^{2}L^{2}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\kappa^{2}r^{2}}{L^{4}\lambda^{4}}\asymp\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{2}n^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}^{2}}{\|\bm{Q}\|^{2}}\gtrsim\frac{\sigma^{2}n\|(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}}{\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{2}n^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}}\gtrsim\frac{\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda^{4}L^{2}}}{\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{2}n^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}}\asymp\frac{1}{\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}n},

which diverges by (
11
).

Combining our results for
T_{1}
,
T_{2}
, and
T_{3}
, we have proven (
67
).
We now prove (
68
). We will first bound the quantity
\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{4}
. We recall that

\displaystyle Y_{t}=A_{t}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{2}-\sigma^{2})+B_{t}\bm{Z}_{t},

where
B_{t}
can be written as
\sum_{s<t}a_{s}\bm{Z}_{s}
, where
a_{s}
are deterministic coefficients on each
\bm{Z}_{s}
. Thus, by subgaussianity, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{4}

\displaystyle=A_{t}^{4}\bigg(\sigma_{8}^{8}-4\sigma^{2}\sigma_{6}^{6}+6\sigma^{4}\sigma_{4}^{4}-3\sigma^{8}\bigg)+6A_{t}^{2}(\sigma_{4}^{4}-\sigma^{4})\sigma^{2}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}+\sigma_{4}^{4}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{8}A_{t}^{4}+\sigma^{6}A_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}+\sigma^{4}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{4}.

(69)

We first compute
\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{4}
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[B_{t}^{4}]=\begin{cases}\sigma_{4}^{4}\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}16(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{4}+\sum_{k_{2}<i}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll})^{4}+\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{4}\Bigg]\\
\qquad+6\sigma^{4}\bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l,\,l_{2}^{\prime}<l_{2}}16(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\\
\qquad+\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}2(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}2(c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\\
\qquad+\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}<i,\,k_{2}^{\prime}<k_{2}}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}<i}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\\
\qquad+\sum_{k_{2}<i}\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}<i}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l,\,(k_{2}^{\prime},l_{2}^{\prime})<(k_{2},l_{2})}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{ik_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\bigg],&k=i\\
\sigma_{4}^{4}\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{4}+\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{4}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{4}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}<l}16(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{4}\Bigg]\\
\quad+6\sigma^{4}\Bigg[\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l,\,l_{2}^{\prime}<l_{2}}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\frac{1}{2}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}\\
\quad+\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}+\sum_{l_{2}<l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{l_{2}^{\prime}<l}4(c_{kk}^{ll_{2}}+c_{ii}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\\
\quad+\sum_{l_{3}<l_{3}^{\prime},\,l_{3},l_{3}^{\prime}\geq l}\frac{1}{4}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}^{\prime}l})^{2}+\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\sum_{k_{2}<k}\frac{1}{2}(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}\\
\quad+\sum_{l_{3}\geq l}\sum_{k_{2}<k,l_{2}<l}2(c_{ik}^{l_{3}l})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}<k,k_{2}^{\prime}<k_{2}}(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll})^{2}\\
\quad+\sum_{k_{2}<k}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}<k,l_{2}<l}4(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}<k,k_{2}^{\prime}<k\\
l_{2}<l,l_{2}^{\prime}<l,(k_{2}^{\prime},l_{2}^{\prime})<(k_{2},l_{2})\end{subarray}}16(c_{kk_{2}}^{ll_{2}})^{2}(c_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}^{ll_{2}^{\prime}})^{2}\Bigg],&k<i.\end{cases}

Note that each term above consists of at most
O(n^{2}L^{2})
terms, each of which are bounded by
\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{4}
.
We now sum this over
t
; i.e., over
k\leq i
and
l\leq L
, which has at most
O(n^{2}L)
terms, giving

\displaystyle\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{4}\lesssim n^{4}L^{3}\sigma^{4}\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{4}.

We recall that from the definition of the coefficients
c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}}
we have that

\displaystyle\big|c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}}\big|

\displaystyle=\bigg|e_{i}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{R}^{(l_{1})}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}(\bm{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{R}}^{\top})^{-1}\bm{R}^{(l_{2})}\bm{U}^{\top}e_{k}\bigg|\lesssim\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\frac{\kappa^{2}r}{\lambda^{2}L^{2}}.

We also note that from our previous calculation
\sigma^{2}\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}\lesssim s^{2}
. We further recall that by the definition of
A_{t}
, we have that
\sum_{t}A_{t}^{4}\lesssim n^{2}L\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{4}
. Thus, combining these observations with (
69
), we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}Y_{t}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{8}n^{2}L\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{4}+\sigma^{6}\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{2}\sum_{t}\mathbb{E}B_{t}^{2}+n^{4}L^{3}\sigma^{8}\max_{i,k,l_{1},l_{2}}(c_{ik}^{l_{1}l_{2}})^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{8}n^{2}L\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{8}\frac{\kappa^{8}r^{4}}{\lambda^{8}L^{8}}+\sigma^{4}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{4}}s^{2}+n^{4}L^{4}\sigma^{8}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{8}\frac{\kappa^{8}r^{4}}{\lambda^{8}L^{8}}

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{2}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{4}}+\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{8}\frac{\sigma^{8}n^{4}r^{4}\kappa^{8}}{\lambda^{8}L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{4}\bigg(\frac{L^{2}\lambda^{4}}{nr\sigma^{4}}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{4}\frac{\sigma^{4}\kappa^{4}r^{2}}{\lambda^{4}L^{4}}+\frac{L^{4}\lambda^{8}}{n^{2}r^{2}\sigma^{8}}\|\bm{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{8}\frac{\sigma^{8}n^{4}r^{4}\kappa^{8}}{\lambda^{8}L^{4}}\bigg)
